  110:     public RuntimeControlCommand buildCommand(RuntimeControlActionReqVO req, RuntimeControlProperties properties) {
  111:         boolean linuxBackup = isBackupAction() && properties.getBackupOps().isLinuxLocal();
  112:         List<String> args = linuxBackup
  113:                 ? linuxBackupArguments(action, req, properties)
  114:                 : new ArrayList<>(argumentsBuilder.apply(req));
  115:         if (deploysReleasePackage()) {
  116:             appendRemoteDeployTargetArguments(args, resolveEnvironment(req), properties);
  117:         }
  118:         if (this == APPLY_TEST_DB_SQL) {
  119:             appendApplyTestDbSqlTargetArguments(args, properties);
  120:         }
  121:         if (this == BUILD_RELEASE || deploysReleasePackage()) {
  122:             appendReleaseTargetHostArguments(args, properties, "prod".equals(resolveEnvironment(req)));
  123:         }
  124:         if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(req.enableSmartReleaseReport())) {
  125:             if (!supportsSmartReleaseReport()) {
  126:                 throw new RuntimeControlException("动作不支持智能发布报告: " + action);
  127:             }
  128:             args.add("-EnableSmartReleaseReport");
  129:         }
  130:         if (isBackupAction() && !linuxBackup) {
  131:             args.add("-OperatorName");
  132:             args.add(properties.getOperatorId());
  133:         }
  134:         String script = linuxBackup
  135:                 ? requireText(properties.getBackupOps().getLinuxScriptPath(), "backupOps.linuxScriptPath")
  136:                 : scriptPath;
  137:         return new RuntimeControlCommand(resolveEnvironment(req), action, script, args);
  138:     }

  182:     public void validateRequest(RuntimeControlActionReqVO req) {

  405:     public List<RuntimeControlReleasePackageRespVO> getReleasePackages() {
  406:         RuntimeControlNasBrowser.NasConfig nasConfig = releaseNasConfig();
  407:         return nasBrowser.listDirectories(nasConfig).stream()
  408:                 .map(entry -> buildReleasePackageResponse(nasConfig, entry))
  409:                 .filter(item -> STATUS_AVAILABLE.equals(item.status()))
  410:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(RuntimeControlReleasePackageRespVO::builtAt,
  411:                                 Comparator.nullsLast(Comparator.reverseOrder()))
  412:                         .thenComparing(RuntimeControlReleasePackageRespVO::releaseTag, Comparator.reverseOrder()))
  413:                 .toList();
  414:     }

  416:     public RuntimeControlReleaseStatusRespVO getReleaseStatus() {
  417:         List<RuntimeControlReleasePackageRespVO> releasePackages = getReleasePackages();
  418:         RuntimeControlOverviewRespVO overview = getOverview();
  419:         RuntimeControlNasBrowser.NasConfig nasConfig = releaseNasConfig();
  420:         List<RuntimeControlOperationRespVO> recentReleaseOperations = getOperations().stream()
  421:                 .filter(operation -> List.of("build-release", "publish-test", "mark-release-tested",
  422:                         "promote-prod", "promote-backup").contains(operation.action()))
  423:                 .limit(10)
  424:                 .toList();
  425:         String latestTestedReleaseTag = releasePackages.stream()
  426:                 .filter(item -> Boolean.TRUE.equals(item.tested()))
  427:                 .map(RuntimeControlReleasePackageRespVO::releaseTag)
  428:                 .findFirst()
  429:                 .orElse(null);
  430:         String testCurrentReleaseTag = resolveCurrentReleaseTag(overview, "test");
  431:         String prodCurrentReleaseTag = resolveCurrentReleaseTag(overview, "prod");
  432:         String backupCurrentReleaseTag = resolveCurrentReleaseTag(overview, "backup");
  433:         return new RuntimeControlReleaseStatusRespVO(releasePackages, overview.statuses(), recentReleaseOperations,
  434:                 testCurrentReleaseTag,
  435:                 prodCurrentReleaseTag,
  436:                 backupCurrentReleaseTag,
  437:                 buildCurrentReleaseNotes(nasConfig, releasePackages, "test", testCurrentReleaseTag),
  438:                 buildCurrentReleaseNotes(nasConfig, releasePackages, "prod", prodCurrentReleaseTag),
  439:                 buildCurrentReleaseNotes(nasConfig, releasePackages, "backup", backupCurrentReleaseTag),
  440:                 latestTestedReleaseTag);
  441:     }

  571:     public synchronized PageResult<RuntimeControlAlertRespVO> getAlertsPage() {
  572:         return PageResult.of(readStateList("alerts.json", RuntimeControlAlertRespVO.class).stream()
  573:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(RuntimeControlAlertRespVO::createdAt,
  574:                         Comparator.nullsLast(Comparator.reverseOrder())))
  575:                 .toList());

  577: 
  578:     public synchronized RuntimeControlAlertRespVO createAlert(RuntimeControlAlertCreateReqVO req) {
  579:         Map<String, Object> templateParams = new LinkedHashMap<>();
  580:         if (req.templateParams() != null) {
  581:             templateParams.putAll(req.templateParams());
  582:         }
  583:         templateParams.putIfAbsent("environment", req.environment());
  584:         templateParams.putIfAbsent("action", req.action());
  585:         templateParams.putIfAbsent("severity", req.severity());
  586:         templateParams.putIfAbsent("title", req.title());
  587:         templateParams.putIfAbsent("content", req.content());
  588: 
  589:         String failureReason = resolveAlertBlockReason(req.environment(), req.action(), req.notifyTemplateCode());
  590:         RuntimeControlAlertRespVO alert = new RuntimeControlAlertRespVO(
  591:                 nextStateId("alerts.json", RuntimeControlAlertRespVO.class, RuntimeControlAlertRespVO::id),
  592:                 trimToEmpty(req.environment()), trimToEmpty(req.action()), trimToEmpty(req.severity()),
  593:                 trimToEmpty(req.title()), trimToEmpty(req.content()), trimToEmpty(req.notifyTemplateCode()),
  594:                 templateParams, STATUS_BLOCKED, null, failureReason, Instant.now(), null, null, null);
  595:         return saveStateItem("alerts.json", RuntimeControlAlertRespVO.class, RuntimeControlAlertRespVO::id, alert);
  596:     }

 1014:     private void validateReleasePackageAvailability(RuntimeControlOperationAction action, String releaseTag) {
 1015:         if (action != RuntimeControlOperationAction.PUBLISH_TEST
 1016:                 && action != RuntimeControlOperationAction.PROMOTE_PROD
 1017:                 && action != RuntimeControlOperationAction.PROMOTE_BACKUP) {
 1018:             return;
 1019:         }

 1021:                 .filter(item -> releaseTag.equals(item.releaseTag()) || releaseTag.equals(item.packageDirectoryName()))

 1023:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeControlException("releaseTag 发布包缺少 manifest/checksum 或不存在: " + releaseTag));

 1025:                 || action == RuntimeControlOperationAction.PROMOTE_BACKUP)

 1027:             if (action != RuntimeControlOperationAction.PROMOTE_BACKUP
 1028:                     || !hasSuccessfulCodeOnlyTestDeployment(releasePackage)) {
 1029:                 throw new RuntimeControlException("releaseTag 尚未测试通过: " + releaseTag);
 1030:             }
 1031:         }
 1032:         if (action == RuntimeControlOperationAction.PROMOTE_BACKUP
 1033:                 && hasText(releasePackage.testedRecoverySetCandidateId())) {
 1034:             RuntimeControlRestoreCandidateRespVO candidate =
 1035:                     requireAvailableRestoreCandidate(releasePackage.testedRecoverySetCandidateId());

 1041:             }
 1042:         }
 1043:     }

 2193:     }
 2194: 
 2195:     private void guardWrite(String environment, String reason, String prodConfirmText, boolean productionGuard,
 2196:                             boolean requireProdConfirmText) {
 2197:         if (reason == null || reason.isBlank()) {
 2198:             throw new RuntimeControlException("操作原因 reason is required");
 2199:         }
 2200:         if (!properties.getEnvironments().containsKey(environment)) {
 2201:             if (productionGuard) {
 2202:                 throw new RuntimeControlException("生产级动作目标环境不存在: " + environment);
 2203:             }
 2204:             return;
 2205:         }
 2206:         RuntimeControlProperties.Environment targetEnvironment = properties.requireEnvironment(environment);
 2207:         if (!targetEnvironment.isAccessEnabled()) {
 2208:             throw new RuntimeControlException("环境未授权执行写动作: " + firstText(targetEnvironment.getAccessDisabledReason(), environment));
 2209:         }
 2210:         if (productionGuard) {
 2211:             if (!properties.isProductionWriteEnabled()) {
 2212:                 throw new RuntimeControlException("正式服/生产级写动作默认禁用，需显式启用 production-write-enabled");
 2213:             }
 2214:             if (requireProdConfirmText && !PROD_CONFIRM_TEXT.equals(prodConfirmText)) {
 2215:                 throw new RuntimeControlException("生产级动作必须提交确认文案 PROD");
 2216:             }
 2217:         }

--- around line 2188 ---
 2180:             double usagePercent = total <= 0 ? 0D : (used * 100D / total);
 2181:             return new RuntimeControlStorageMetricRespVO(code, name, "PASS", used, usable, usagePercent, null);
 2182:         } catch (IOException ex) {
